# Strategy Validation Matrix

Scope: all implemented TTrades strategies, all available assets and reasonable timeframes.

Data cap per dataset for this pass: 20000 bars.

Mode: FAST

Optimization: if naive (no fee/slippage) is not tradable, realism sweeps are skipped.

Units: crypto net is `%`; futures net is `$` per 1 micro contract (MNQ=$2/pt, MES=$5/pt, GOLD=$10/pt).

## MNQ Strict BAG Update (Fractal Alignment)

- Added strict `real_bag_only` validation path for MNQ fractal alignment with realism constraints.
- Result: strict mode improves quality in isolated pockets but remains too sparse for promotion at meaningful trade-count floors.
- Trade-count gate checks (`min-trades` 30/50/100) did not produce promotable strict candidates.
- Detailed notes: `reports/strategy_overviews/MNQ_FRACTAL_ALIGNMENT_STRICT_REPORT.md`
